## Technology Blog

<a id="item-tech-blog-1"></a>
### [Jimmish vs. Spikish Movements: An Emotional Framework](https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/niJbjrrvKtLAPo8Rb/pasta-marketing-magic-players-and-political-movements) ⭐️ 8.0/10

rss · LessWrong AI风险社区 · Sep 20, 22:33

**「Background」** The author challenges Richard Ngo’s categorization of political groups by arguing that the distinction between the Tech Left/Right and the Woke Left is not primarily about opposing goals, but about fundamentally different psychological motivations.

**「Solution」** Drawing on pasta sauce market segmentation and Magic: The Gathering archetypes, the author proposes a new binary: &\#x27;spikish&\#x27; movements, which treat politics as a strategic tool to achieve a concrete goal, and &\#x27;jimmish&\#x27; movements, which use politics as a direct outlet for emotional impulses. While spikish groups \(like the Tech Left\) successfully transmute empathy into a world-model and actionable policy, jimmish groups often bypass this transmutation, turning impulsive outputs into highly optimized feedback loops that satisfy the impulse directly rather than achieving a coherent external result. The author argues that spikish movements frequently struggle against jimmish counterparts from the same impulse because they mistakenly project their own goal-oriented rationality onto the emotional, less predictable jimmish behavior.

**「Takeaway」** The central axis of political divergence is not the ultimate goal, but whether a movement’s actions are driven by a reasoned world-model or by a direct outlet for emotional impulses. Recognizing this difference allows observers to correctly predict why seemingly like-minded factions might oppose each other and understand the psychological origins of highly strategic yet counterproductive political stances.

**Tags**: `#political psychology`, `#concepts`, `#magic-the-gathering`, `#marketing`, `#rationality`

<a id="item-tech-blog-4"></a>
### [Personal Appeal for Chinese Outreach in AI Safety](https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/GoX3uYQ4QN5HKvL7u/please-give-them-a-chance-on-china-rationalism-and-ai-safety) ⭐️ 3.0/10

rss · LessWrong AI风险社区 · Sep 20, 18:44

**「Background」** LessWrong author gzjw, a Chinese graduate who discovered rationalism through HPMOR and The Sequences, observes that AI safety has recently become a major topic in China but is currently dominated by one-sided attacks and distorted portrayals. The core challenge is that China&\#x27;s hundreds of millions of educated young people know almost nothing about rationalism or alignment, and the author fears that public opinion is hardening into a negative consensus that will be too costly to reverse.

**「Solution」** The author outlines a personal, full-time commitment to translating and localizing rationalist materials for Chinese readers to reduce friction and counter negative narratives. Specifically, they built an automated workflow to typeset the HPMOR comic into multilingual editable drafts and developed a frontend that allows Chinese users to browse AI-translated rationalist webpages with on-demand glossaries, topic categories, search, and curated reading guides. The author cites that 30% of LessWrong users found the site through HPMOR and that 80% of new readers rated it positively, suggesting a proven onboarding channel. With $15,000 in savings to cover living expenses and a goal to quit their job by February 2027, the author argues that even non-technical individuals can contribute by acting as a bridge between English-language rationalist communities and the Chinese public sphere, leveraging AI tools to make cultural exchange possible despite language barriers.

**「Takeaway」** The author argues that because China produces tens of millions of capable graduates annually who lack exposure to rationalist and AI safety discourse, it is strategically and morally urgent for the international community to provide accessible, positive channels of communication. By giving these individuals a chance to engage with accurate information, the AI safety field gains both a larger talent pool and vital public support, which can prevent entrenched negative consensus and ultimately offer humanity a better chance at a safe future.

**Tags**: `#AI Safety Outreach`, `#Chinese Community`, `#Personal Essay`, `#Translation`, `#Public Perception`

<a id="item-tech-blog-10"></a>
### [Building Recovery: A Personal Guide to Managing Depression](https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/oqtZim8BAirHBLDT4/what-i-ve-learned-about-depression-so-far) ⭐️ ?/10

rss · LessWrong AI风险社区 · Sep 20, 14:27

**「Background」** The author developed this guide as a personal resource to manage chronic depression, recognizing that complex interventions are often overwhelming during low-mood states. The central challenge was creating a framework that bridges the gap between the desire for holistic wellness and the practical exhaustion of mental illness.

**「Solution」** The author proposes a foundational strategy of &quot;tiny habits&quot; that reduce cognitive load by removing the need for willpower in essential bodily functions like sleep, nutrition, and exercise. The guide emphasizes that biological factors, such as vitamin deficiencies and hormonal disruptions, often compound psychological symptoms, suggesting that physical optimization is a prerequisite for mental recovery. While the author found some support groups ineffective, they highlight the value of cognitive behavioral tools, like chatbots, for identifying and reframing entrenched negative thought patterns. Notably, the post itself was generated using a voice-to-text workflow with an AI assistant, a method the author found more sustainable than traditional typing when managing fatigue. This approach treats the reader&\#x27;s own experience as an experiment, encouraging curiosity over self-criticism when implementing these small, incremental changes.

**「Takeaway」** Recovery from depression is most effectively managed by prioritizing the smallest possible physical actions and integrating biological factors into the mental health strategy. By treating one&\#x27;s well-being as a series of manageable experiments rather than a series of monumental tasks, individuals can gradually rebuild their foundational health.

### [AI Systems Fail to Recognize Simple Geometric Shapes and Basic Objects](https://www.faz.net/aktuell/wissen/computer-mathematik/warum-ki-formen-schlechter-erkennt-accg-201244177.html) ⭐️ 7.0/10

A new study reveals that current artificial intelligence systems struggle to recognize even the simplest geometric outlines and basic shapes. The research highlights that these models can produce nonsensical classifications, such as interpreting a cat as a crossword puzzle. This fundamental limitation in computer vision is particularly concerning for the development of autonomous driving technologies, which rely on robust object detection to navigate environments safely.

rss · 法兰克福汇报\(Frankfurter Allgemeine Zeitung \(FAZ\)\) · Sep 20, 07:33

**「The Pareidolia Effect in AI」** Pareidolia is the human psychological phenomenon of seeing meaningful patterns, such as animal faces, in random or ambiguous stimuli, a trait that can occasionally manifest in artificial intelligence systems when they overfit contextual clues in computer vision. Recognizing basic geometric outlines and categorizing objects by shape rather than surface texture are fundamental prerequisites for robust computer vision, a capability that autonomous driving systems rely on to distinguish road infrastructure, traffic signs, and physical obstacles from complex environmental backgrounds.

**「Consequence for Autonomous Driving」** The inability of AI systems to accurately perceive basic shapes and objects poses a significant safety risk for autonomous vehicles, potentially leading to misinterpretations of critical environmental elements. This highlights a critical gap in the robustness required for AI systems operating in complex, real-world driving scenarios.

<details><summary>References</summary>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://arxiv.org/html/2506.20616v4">Shape2Animal: Creative Animal Generation from Natural Silhouettes</a></li>

**「Policy Consequences」** The sustained public mobilization may force legislators to reconsider or amend the immunity clauses to avoid alienating a significant portion of the electorate. <a id="item-tech-news-124"></a>
### [UNIDO Chief: Multilateral Partnerships Crucial for Fair Global Development Outcomes](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182236.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Gert Müller, Director General of the UN Industrial Development Organization \(UNIDO\), emphasized in a recent interview that multilateralism and international cooperation are critical for addressing rising global uncertainties. Müller highlighted the strong alignment between China&\#x27;s Global Development Initiative and the UN&\#x27;s 2030 Sustainable Development Agenda, noting that both prioritize development, practical action, and deepened partnership. He showcased the integration of Chinese technology and experience with UNIDO&\#x27;s multilateral platform through new mechanisms, such as the China-Africa-UN cooperation center and the BRICS industrial capability center. Looking ahead, Müller expressed UNIDO&\#x27;s intent to utilize the Global Industrial AI Alliance to share artificial intelligence benefits with the Global South, aiming to narrow the global intelligence and development gaps.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Development Initiatives and Institutions」** The Global Development Initiative \(GDI\) was proposed by China&\#x27;s President in September 2021 to refocus the international community on development challenges. UNIDO is the specialized UN agency tasked with promoting sustainable industrial development and international industrial cooperation. The Global South refers to the developing regions, primarily in Africa, Asia, and Latin America, which are central to these multilateral development efforts.

**「Global Development Benefits」** Developing nations in the Global South will gain access to shared artificial intelligence technologies and sustainable industrialization frameworks, enabling them to build localized production capacities and accelerate their participation in the global economy. Multilateral partnerships will provide more equitable pathways for achieving sustainable development goals and bridging the digital and industrial divide.

<a id="item-tech-news-126"></a>
### [Hong Kong&\#x27;s First Chief Executive, Donald Tsang, Dies at 89](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182240.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Donald Tsang, a pioneering figure in the practice of &\#x27;One Country, Two Systems&\#x27; and the first Chief Executive of the Hong Kong Special Administrative Region, passed away in Hong Kong on September 8, 2026, at the age of 89. A distinguished patriot, business leader, and politician, Tsang served as the 1st and 2nd Chief Executive of Hong Kong from 1997 to 2008, and later held the position of Vice Chairman of the Chinese People&\#x27;s Political Consultative Conference. The People&\#x27;s Daily published a detailed obituary, highlighting his significant contributions to China&\#x27;s economic development, his leadership during the Asian Financial Crisis, and his unwavering support for national sovereignty and security. He is also credited with co-founding the US-China Institute and the Building a Better Hong Kong Foundation to foster cross-strait and international dialogue.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Context」** Donald Tsang was the first to hold the office of Chief Executive of Hong Kong, a role created under the Basic Law to transition the territory&\#x27;s governance from British to Chinese sovereignty in 1997. As the &\#x27;Father of the Basic Law&\#x27; alongside Dr. Tsang, his work was central to implementing the &\#x27;One Country, Two Systems&\#x27; principle.

**「Significance」** His death marks the passing of a key historical figure whose work fundamentally shaped the institutional and political framework of modern Hong Kong. He will be remembered as a central architect of the Special Administrative Region&\#x27;s early governance and international integration.

<a id="item-tech-news-132"></a>
### [China Advances Ecological Product Valuation and Credit Markets in Fujian](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182190.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Fujian Province&\#x27;s Jiangle County released a valuation report for 100,000 mu of carbon sink reserve forests and used the quantified results to sign a VEP \(Valuation of Ecosystem Products\) credit agreement with a bank. This move translates ecological assets into financial collateral, demonstrating the practical application of China&\#x27;s policy that ecosystems can be monetized. Last year, Fujian created a three-tier catalog of ecological products across six major ecosystems to standardize valuation. The province has also piloted other financial tools, such as VEP loans in Wuyishan and Yong&\#x27;an counties, to create a market for ecological services like water conservation and biodiversity protection.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Understanding Ecosystem Value Realization」** Ecosystem Product Value \(VEP\) refers to the monetizable worth of ecological services, such as water retention and carbon sequestration, treated as tradable assets with defined property rights. This framework allows natural resources to be priced and utilized in markets, enabling governments and financial institutions to support ecological protection through economic incentives rather than solely public expenditure.

**「Impact」** For local governments and banks in Fujian, these mechanisms provide a new financial channel to fund ecological conservation by converting natural resources into tradable assets. This model may encourage other regions to adopt similar valuation and credit frameworks, although its scalability depends on standardizing ecological product markets nationwide.

This People&\#x27;s Daily article, published on September 21, 2026, profiles Wang Miaoli, a senior police officer at the Henan Province Second Compulsory Isolation and Drug Rehabilitation Center, who recounted how he intervened to save a child from a knife-wielding attacker during the New Year&\#x27;s Day holiday. The piece emphasizes his long-term work in drug rehabilitation, where he has helped over 1,200 individuals recover through personalized education and psychological support. The 2026 World Manufacturing Conference \(WMC\) opened in Hefei, Anhui Province, on September 20, 2026. Vice Chairman of the Standing Committee of the National People’s Congress, Wang Dongming, attended the opening and delivered a speech, affirming that manufacturing is the foundation of the real economy and the cornerstone of a strong nation. Wang urged global cooperation to advance the intelligent, green, and integrated development of manufacturing, emphasizing specific drivers such as leveraging scientific and technological innovation, using artificial intelligence to build smart manufacturing advantages, promoting low-carbon transition, and expanding open cooperation. Under the theme “Smart Manufacturing the World, Creating a Better Life,” the event drew over 2,000 participants from more than 50 countries and regions.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「World Manufacturing Convention」** The World Manufacturing Convention \(WMC\) is an annual international industry event held in Hefei, Anhui Province, China, which gathers global business leaders, government officials, and technology experts to discuss trends in manufacturing. The event typically features multi-day programs of business engagement, technology showcases, and discussions focused on the industry&\#x27;s future direction.

**「Impact」** The conference provides a multi-national platform for aligning strategies around AI-driven smart manufacturing and green transition, facilitating direct industrial cooperation and setting policy directions for global supply chains.

### [Ironing out legal paths for vocational education in Zhejiang](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182245.html) ⭐️ ?/10

From September 15 to 18, 2026, Vice Chairman of the Standing Committee of the National People&\#x27;s Congress Tie Ning led a vocational education law enforcement inspection team to conduct checks in Zhejiang Province. She emphasized the need to fully and effectively implement the Vocational Education Law, accelerate the construction of a modern vocational education system, and promote high-quality development on the rule of law track. The inspection team commended Zhejiang&\#x27;s achievements in implementing the law, highlighting the importance of clarifying the responsibilities of all parties, improving supporting regulations, and enhancing legal publicity to build a supportive social consensus. The team held symposiums and conducted on-site inspections at vocational institutions to address hot-button issues and ensure the law&\#x27;s provisions are effectively enforced.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Background」** The Vocational Education Law in China, revised and implemented in 2022, establishes the legal framework for vocational education, emphasizing its status as a major category of education and not a second-choice path. Periodic enforcement inspections by the NPC Standing Committee are a constitutional mechanism to monitor the implementation of national laws, ensuring they are effectively enforced across provinces and addressing systemic challenges.

**「Impact」** For vocational education institutions and local governments in Zhejiang, the inspection underscores the need to strengthen local supporting regulations and legal awareness to resolve persistent issues and improve the quality of vocational training programs. It signals a national priority to align provincial practices with the new legal standards, potentially influencing future policy adjustments in other regions.

### [National Moral Role Model Event Held in Wenzhou](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182246.html) ⭐️ ?/10

On September 20, 2026, the Central Office of Spiritual Civilization Construction hosted the &\#x27;Virtue Illuminates China, Role Models Near Us&\#x27; thematic event in Wenzhou, Zhejiang. The event featured nationally recognized moral model representatives presenting their stories through situational skits, short musical dramas, and video displays to highlight acts of mutual assistance, bravery, integrity, dedication, and familial devotion. Specific stories highlighted included Wenzhou migrant worker Li Xuesheng, who died rescuing a child on a railway track, and physician Chen Jie leading public-interest medical campaigns. The following day, participants visited the Wenzhou Moral Hall for further study. This event is part of a nationwide series of exchanges organized by the central office this year to foster positive social values.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Background」** The &\#x27;Virtue Illuminates China&\#x27; campaign is a regular moral education initiative in China that showcases the deeds of ordinary citizens and public officials who exemplify traditional virtues. These events typically use artistic performances to make abstract moral concepts accessible and inspiring to a broader audience.

<a id="item-tech-news-139"></a>
### [Guilin, Guangxi Advances Red Tourism and Rural Revival Through Long March Heritage](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182247.html) ⭐️ ?/10

In Guangxi Zhuang Autonomous Region, Guilin has deepened its efforts to preserve and promote the legacy of the 1934 Xiang River Campaign, the pivotal episode of the Chinese Red Army&\#x27;s Long March. By 2025, the region had cultivated over 100 young &\#x27;Red Scarf&\#x27; guides at the memorial park, which by mid-2026 had welcomed more than 11 million visitors. A dedicated &\#x27;Red Xiang River&\#x27; tourist bus launched in April 2025 linked key historical sites in the north, transporting over 150,000 passengers by the end of 2026. This red tourism infrastructure has actively driven local agricultural modernization, as seen in Maoshan Village, where grape cultivation expanded to over ten varieties and per-acre income rose to over 10,000 yuan, and in Lushui Village, where a locally named &\#x27;Breakthrough Peach&\#x27; variety now yields a gross profit of 13,000 to 15,000 yuan per acre.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Context of the Xiang River Campaign」** The Xiang River Campaign of 1934 was a critical and costly battle during the Long March, where the Central Red Army lost tens of thousands of soldiers while attempting to break through Nationalist military encirclement. The region&\#x27;s red tourism initiative is part of a broader national effort to inherit the &\#x27;Long March spirit&\#x27; by combining historical site preservation with rural economic development and cultural education.

**「Economic and Cultural Outcomes」** For local communities in northern Guangxi, the development of red tourism has become a core economic driver, directly linking historical preservation to increased agricultural yields, diversified product offerings, and annual tourist footfalls exceeding 300,000 in some villages. This model provides a practical template for integrating cultural heritage with agricultural and lodging industries to accelerate rural revitalization in historically significant old revolutionary base areas.

---

The Ministry of Culture and Tourism announced details for two major national arts events scheduled for late 2026. The 8th China Poetry Festival will run from October 12 to 23 in Taizhou, Zhejiang Province, under the theme &\#x27;Poetry Surges in Mountains and Seas, Poetic China.&\#x27; Its activities include opening and closing ceremonies, academic symposiums, creative workshops, mass cultural events, and &\#x27;poetry plus&\#x27; cultural-tourism integration programs. Separately, the 11th China Peking Opera Arts Festival will be held in Wuhan, Hubei Province, from October 15 to November 5. The Peking Opera festival features seven main activities, including excellent repertoire performances, small-theater shows, school exhibitions, and a youth talent creation camp, alongside community outreach and themed art exhibitions.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「National Arts Festivals」** The China Poetry Festival and China Peking Opera Festival are periodic national-level cultural events organized under the Ministry of Culture and Tourism. These festivals are held to promote traditional Chinese literature and performing arts through showcases, academic discussions, and public engagement.

**「Impact」** The host cities of Taizhou and Wuhan will see concentrated boosts to local cultural tourism and public engagement over consecutive October weeks as major national audiences and art institutions attend the scheduled events.

<a id="item-tech-news-142"></a>
### [Twenty-Sixth National History Academic Annual Conference Held](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182250.html) ⭐️ ?/10

The Twenty-Sixth Annual Academic Conference on Modern Chinese History was held in Hefei, Anhui Province, from September 19 to 20, 2026. The conference theme was &quot;The Theory and Practice of Establishing and Practicing a Correct View of Merit,&quot; integrating this concept with research on modern Chinese history. More than 80 experts and scholars from relevant fields participated in in-depth discussions. Scholars emphasized that a correct view of merit combines value and institutional attributes, enabling the transformation of ideological guidance into effective governance. The event aimed to advance the development of modern Chinese historical research by focusing on merit evaluation, practical pathways, and institutional design to build an independent knowledge system for China&\#x27;s national history studies. The conference was jointly organized by the Chinese Association for the Study of Modern Chinese History, the Institute of Contemporary China, the Anhui Academy of Social Sciences, and Anhui University.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Context」** The annual conference is a prominent academic event in China focused on the study of modern Chinese history, organized by leading institutions in the field. The concept of a &quot;correct view of merit&quot; refers to an approach to public service that prioritizes long-term, sustainable achievements over short-term, measurable gains, and is a key emphasis in current Chinese governance discourse. Integrating political and governance concepts into historical research reflects a broader academic trend of linking historical analysis with contemporary policy and institutional development.

**「Impact」** For scholars and researchers in modern Chinese history, the conference signals a continued emphasis on aligning historical inquiry with state policy priorities, potentially shaping research agendas, funding directions, and the development of an independent Chinese academic knowledge framework in the field.

<a id="item-tech-news-143"></a>
### [China Reports Growth in Science Popularization Staff and Funding for 2025](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182251.html) ⭐️ ?/10

China&\#x27;s Ministry of Science and Technology released 2025 national science popularization statistics, indicating steady growth in personnel, funding, and infrastructure. The country had 2.25 million full-time and part-time science popularization professionals, up 1.72% from 2024, and 13.71 million registered volunteers. Total funding for science popularization reached 24.398 billion yuan, a 9.80% increase, with per capita special funding rising by 0.41 yuan to 6.01 yuan. The number of science museums and technology centers increased by 98 to 1,988, and visitor attendance grew by 10.96% to 338 million visits. Online dissemination expanded significantly, with 1.11 million WeChat public accounts, 49.236 billion video views, and over 162,000 lectures attracting 2.201 billion participants.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Background」** Science popularization, or popularization of scientific knowledge, is a government-supported initiative in China aimed at improving public scientific literacy through education, museums, media, and community activities. The Ministry of Science and Technology publishes annual statistical reports to track the development of this sector, including workforce size, funding allocation, and public participation metrics.

**「Impact」** The data indicates that public institutions and media platforms in China have significantly expanded their science communication reach, particularly through digital channels and offline events, reflecting a sustained governmental priority on enhancing scientific literacy across the population.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-144"></a>
### [China Mandates Provincial Rare Disease Clinics by End of 2026](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182252.html) ⭐️ ?/10

The National Health Commission and the National Administration of Chinese Medicine recently issued a notice requiring all provinces, autonomous regions, municipalities, and the Xinjiang Production and Construction Corps to establish rare disease outpatient clinics in at least one provincial-level or higher hospital by the end of 2026. Additionally, prefecture-level cities with populations exceeding three million must launch rare disease clinics in at least one hospital, staffed by relevant specialists to provide standardized diagnosis and treatment services. The directive also mandates strengthening prenatal screening, genetic counseling, and premarital health checks to prevent congenital defects and rare diseases. The policy encourages qualified medical institutions to construct rare disease wards and utilize multidisciplinary and integrated traditional Chinese-Western medicine approaches for comprehensive patient care.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

<a id="item-tech-news-147"></a>
### [People&\#x27;s Daily Calls for Action Against Pop-up Ad Exploits](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182205.html) ⭐️ ?/10

People&\#x27;s Daily published a report highlighting how pop-up and &\#x27;shake-to-open&\#x27; ads in common Chinese apps have created significant user annoyance, safety hazards, and financial risks, particularly for the elderly and drivers. The article notes that these ads often use hidden &\#x27;close&\#x27; buttons that trigger further redirections and exploit phone gyroscopes to capture minor movements for advertising revenue. Because such ads rely on third-party SDKs that are cheap to integrate, the article attributes their prevalence to a low-cost, high-reward business model tied to metrics like click-per-cost and action-per-cost. It further points out that the May 2023 Internet Advertising Management Measures set fines of 5,000 to 30,000 RMB, which experts say is too low to deter the industry. The paper notes that on June 9, the Ministry of Industry and Information Technology convened a meeting to direct platforms and device makers to strictly regulate how information windows appear in apps.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Background」** In May 2023, China&\#x27;s State Administration for Market Regulation and Ministry of Industry and Information Technology issued the Internet Advertising Management Measures, a regulation that specifically mandates that pop-up and splash-screen ads must have a prominent, one-click close button. Despite this requirement, many app developers continue to embed third-party ad SDKs that use complex workarounds to bypass the spirit of the rule, shifting the burden of enforcement onto regulators and consumer hotlines like 12315.

**「Impact」** For consumers, this issue manifests as a real-world financial and safety risk, with documented cases of seniors accidentally authorizing passwordless payments or subscribing to services they do not recognize, sometimes losing up to hundreds of RMB to shell companies that are impossible to trace. For the broader digital ecosystem, the article suggests that regulators may escalate penalties from fixed fines to per-violation and multiple-times penalty models, while also introducing OS-level features such as a &quot;one-click disable non-voluntary redirects&quot; toggle to prevent apps from bypassing user intent.

A national subsidy of 800 yuan per month for elderly individuals with moderate or severe care needs cannot be claimed by residents in the Jiamusi National Agricultural High-Tech Zone in Heilongjiang. The zone&\#x27;s administrative council, as a government-sent派出 institution without an administrative division, cannot directly receive or process the subsidy funds. Local officials confirmed that data linkage between the zone&\#x27;s and the adjacent city of Fuchen&\#x27;s civil affairs and finance departments was still not established as of September 14, 2026, despite directives issued in early June. The article notes this case highlights a broader systemic issue where China&\#x27;s development zones face difficulty in coordinating social affairs and services with their local territorial governments due to mismatches in administrative authority and financial responsibilities.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Background」** Since 2017, the Chinese central government has encouraged development zones to focus on industrial and economic growth while delegating social management and public services to territorial governments. However, many zones still retain some social responsibilities or are assigned tasks outside their authorized jurisdiction, leading to inefficiencies and conflicts between zone councils and local governments.

**「Impact」** Families of elderly residents in Jiamusi&\#x27;s Agricultural High-Tech Zone are unable to access the national care subsidy, leaving them to bear the full cost of around 3,000 yuan in monthly care expenses. This specific case is expected to prompt higher-level government intervention to establish a coordinated mechanism between the zone council and territorial civil affairs and finance departments.

Academician Chen Liquan, who began his research on a visit to Germany in 1976, initiated China&\#x27;s foundational studies in solid-state ion and lithium battery technologies despite the field&\#x27;s lack of domestic infrastructure. His team achieved key milestones, including China&\#x27;s first solid-state lithium battery in 1988 and its first independent lithium battery pilot production line in 1998. By 2014, China&\#x27;s lithium battery industry had captured the world&\#x27;s leading share of the global market. Chen&\#x27;s research resolved the global challenge of solid-solid contact in batteries through in-situ solidification methods, securing China&\#x27;s position as a leader in the industry.

**「Strategic Significance」** The foundational research pioneered by Chen and his team directly enabled the global market dominance of China&\#x27;s lithium battery industry, establishing the technical base for the national &quot;Electric China&quot; initiative.

### [Quantum Instrument Startup Progress Report in China](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182224.html) ⭐️ ?/10

He Yu, a 2003 graduate class entrant from the University of Science and Technology of China, founded Guoyi Quantum in 2016 to develop domestic quantum sensors and high-end scientific instruments. The company began in a 14-square-meter office provided by his alma mater, which has since supported its growth. After a decade of focused development, Guoyi Quantum achieved commercial production of an electron paramagnetic resonance spectrometer with an internationally leading signal-to-noise ratio exceeding 10,000. The company&\#x27;s high-end electron microscopes are now reported to enter the international first tier, facilitating the transition of quantum precision measurement from laboratory research to industrial application.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Domestic High-End Instruments and Quantum Sensors」** High-end scientific instruments and quantum sensors are essential tools for probing microscopic structures, measuring weak signals, and advancing research in cells, protein molecules, and quantum materials. Historically, China relied on imported equipment, which faced supply constraints and price volatility, highlighting the national push for achieving technological self-reliance in precision measurement. Guoyi Quantum \(Hefei\) Co., Ltd., founded in 2016 by He Yu, focuses on developing domestic electron spin resonance spectrometers and electron microscopes to reduce this dependence.

**「Practical Impact」** The commercialization of high-sensitivity quantum instruments reduces dependence on imported equipment for microscopic research, enabling domestic laboratories and industrial users to access advanced measurement capabilities. This progress contributes to the broader goal of technological self-reliance in critical scientific infrastructure.

### [Japan&\#x27;s Record Defense Budget Signals Military Expansion](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182127.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Japan&\#x27;s Ministry of Defense announced a 2027 fiscal year budget request of approximately 8.9 trillion yen, setting a new historical high and representing a 60 percent increase since 2022. Analysts predict the final budget may exceed 10 trillion yen, marking a 15th consecutive year of growth under the Takaichi government. The budget emphasizes &\#x27;new combat methods&\#x27; with major investments in long-range missiles, hypersonic weapons, and AI-integrated unmanned systems, shifting from defensive to offensive capabilities. The government has introduced new defense-specific taxes and bonds to fund the expansion, while military manufacturing orders have tripled over five years, reaching 2.69 trillion yen in FY2025. This strategy aims to create a defense-economy cycle, effectively accelerating Japan&\#x27;s remilitarization beyond its traditional &\#x27;exclusive defense&\#x27; policy.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Context」** Since the end of World War II, Japan has maintained a &\#x27;defensive-only&\#x27; posture under its pacifist constitution, keeping defense spending below 1 percent of GDP. This changed in late 2022 when Japan revised its three major security documents, breaking the 30-year convention and accelerating military investment. The expansion now includes lethal weapons development and export, representing a fundamental shift in Japan&\#x27;s post-war security policy.

**「Consequences」** The budget reallocation places significant pressure on Japan&\#x27;s domestic economy through new defense-specific taxes and bonds, while neighboring countries face escalating military threats from Japan&\#x27;s newly-acquired offensive missile and unmanned capabilities. Domestic experts have warned that this militarization strategy is creating an unsustainable financial burden and destabilizing regional security.

The Chongqing Jiulongpo district is pursuing a development model centered on urban-rural integration, east-west coordination, and comprehensive urbanization. Over the past five years, the district&\#x27;s economy has reached 200 billion yuan, with per capita disposable income exceeding 57,000 yuan, and it has built 13 high-quality 15-minute service zones and achieved a 12 billion yuan output in its 2025 urban agriculture sector. A key infrastructure milestone was the opening of the Taojia Tunnel on May 31, 2026, which cut east-west commuting times to under 30 minutes and facilitated resource flow between the dense eastern consumer hubs and the industrial western zones. The district is organizing its growth around four distinct districts, attracting investments such as a 5.2 billion yuan Liyu semiconductor project and a 200,000-ton Southwestern Aluminum recycling line to its western new city area.

**「Geographical and Economic Context of Jialingkou District」** Jialingkou District is a major urban district in Chongqing, but its development has historically been constrained by the Zhongliang Mountain range, which physically divides the area into a densely populated, commerce-heavy east and a less developed, industrial and agricultural west. To address this imbalance, the district established the Chongqing Hub Port Industrial Park in the Jialingkou New City to serve as an economic engine for the western region, aiming to leverage logistics and channel construction to drive regional growth and industrial integration.

**「Impact」** Residents of Jiulongpo district will experience a more integrated quality of life through direct access to improved rural services, a compressed 30-minute east-west commute, and a diversified local economy driven by new industries and cultural tourism clusters.

Authorities are deploying digital twin technology, AI cameras, and structural reinforcement, such as the 2026 upgrade of the 63-ton Ming dynasty bell, to shift heritage management toward proactive, data-driven protection. Simultaneously, the city is activating these sites for contemporary life through the June 2026 opening of the Temple of Heaven Museum, the reopened Zhengyangmen Gate, and public spaces like the Drum Tower that host open-air movie screenings. To coordinate these initiatives, officials published a 2026-2028 action plan integrating intangible cultural heritage and digital tourism across a 61-site national portfolio.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Context on the Beijing Central Axis」** The Beijing Central Axis, a 7.8-kilometer north-south line running through Beijing&\#x27;s Old City, was inscribed on the UNESCO World Heritage List in 2024 after being placed on China&\#x27;s tentative list in 2012. It represents the mature stage of traditional Chinese imperial city planning and serves as a living cultural heritage site that requires ongoing preservation and integration into modern urban life.

<a id="item-tech-news-185"></a>
### [Tusiuan Embroidery Industry in Gansu](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182137.html) ⭐️ ?/10

In Xizhou County, Qinghai, the traditional Tugao embroidery \(Tusi Pankou\) craft has evolved into a local industry valued at 27 million yuan annually. Led by national inheritors like Li Fashu, the craft is now commercialized through a non-heritage workshop established in 2007, and a specialized base launched in 2024. Currently, the county supports 75 inheritors, 5,300 long-term embroiderers, and over 3,000 seasonal embroiderers, with the highest-earning embroiderers earning up to 40,000–50,000 yuan per year. The craft’s “two-needles, two-threads” technique resists machine reproduction, serving as its core competitiveness. Innovative collaborations with universities and brands have expanded the market, launching over 100 products across 8 categories with annual sales exceeding 3 million yuan, and the craft has been showcased at the 2025 Paris exhibition and China International Fashion Week.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Origins of Tu Ethnic Coiling Embroidery」** Tu Ethnic coiling embroidery, or panxiu, is a traditional embroidery technique specific to the Tu people of Huzhu Tu Autonomous County in Qinghai Province, with historical records indicating the craft dates back to at least the 4th century. It was officially recognized and listed in the first batch of China&\#x27;s national intangible cultural heritage lists in 2006. The technique is characterized by its use of a &\#x27;two-needle, two-thread&\#x27; method, which relies on a thick thread for the top coiling and a thin thread for the bottom sewing, making it difficult to replicate with machine production.

**「Rural Economic Impact」** The commercialization of Tu ethnic Tuguxiu embroidery has directly increased rural household incomes, with the highest-earning embroiderers in Huzhu County generating up to 50,000 RMB annually through paid work in local workshops. The external evidence confirms that this &quot;base + enterprise + workshop + farmer&quot; production model has integrated nearly 700 households and over 5,000 registered embroiderers into a sustainable local industry chain.

Over five years, the initiative has evolved from a Chinese policy proposal into a broadly supported international consensus, with China collaborating on more than 2,000 projects and training over 200,000 personnel across more than 120 countries. More than 130 countries and international organizations now support the initiative, and 88 countries belong to its Friends Group. Experts cited highlighted specific achievements, including more than 35 projects jointly implemented with the UNDP that benefited over 18 million people across 24 countries in Asia, Africa, Eastern Europe, and Latin America, and the Bangladesh city poverty program that reached about 387,000 residents.

This initiative emerged as a multilateral framework to address global development deficits, such as financing gaps and infrastructure needs, by emphasizing development-first priorities, inclusivity, and actionable, outcome-oriented cooperation.

**「Concrete impact of the Global Development Initiative」** For developing countries and partner institutions, the Global Development Initiative translates shared development consensus into measurable impact through more than 2,000 jointly implemented cooperation projects, training of over 200,000 individuals, and benefits reaching the populations of 120-plus countries, while UNDP and China have jointly delivered more than 35 funded projects across 24 countries that have directly benefited over 18 million people.

<a id="item-tech-news-190"></a>
### [People&\#x27;s Daily Rebuttal of &\#x27;K-Shaped Differentiation&\#x27; Narrative](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182142.html) ⭐️ ?/10

An editorial in People&\#x27;s Daily argues that applying the &quot;K-shaped differentiation&quot; framework to China&\#x27;s economic transition mischaracterizes phased industrial adjustments as permanent polarization. The author identifies three specific deviations in this interpretation: mistaking localized sectoral pain for systemic decline, undervaluing the upgradability of traditional industries, and ignoring the economy&\#x27;s inherent ability to distribute growth dividends broadly through supply chains. The piece cites China&\#x27;s first-half GDP growth of 4.7% and an urban unemployment rate of 5.2% to demonstrate overall macroeconomic stability. Furthermore, it asserts that China&\#x27;s institutional framework prevents Western-style &\#x27;winner-take-all&\#x27; consolidation by actively supporting small and medium enterprises and equitable income distribution, ensuring that new economic momentum benefits the entire population.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Origins of the K-shaped recovery concept」** The term &quot;K-shaped recovery&quot; was coined by Wall Street institutions following the pandemic, describing an economic pattern where capital concentrates in financial sectors and large tech firms while the middle class and traditional industries deteriorate. This framework, which assumes that such divergence can become self-reinforcing in certain Western institutional settings, has recently become a frequent topic in Chinese public discourse and market analysis. Critics have argued that it is often used to paint a pessimistic picture of structural polarization in China, conflating temporary transition-period imbalances with long-term societal fractures.

**「Policy and market implications」** For domestic policymakers, the editorial reinforces the existing strategic mandate to prevent monopolistic capture of new economic momentum through anti-monopoly enforcement and targeted support for small and medium enterprises. For market participants and international analysts, the framework suggests that China&\#x27;s macroeconomic divergence will remain dynamic and manageable, driven by systemic redistribution rather than irreversible, rigid stratification.

<a id="item-tech-news-191"></a>
### [Qixin&\#x27;s Rural Insurance Clues Reveal Governance Shifts and Gaps](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182143.html) ⭐️ ?/10

A survey of Qixin County in Henan reveals that 6,640 &\#x27;Comprehensive Social Governance Household Property Insurance&\#x27; \(综治家财险\) claims paid between January 2025 and June 2026 highlight significant shifts in rural risk and governance. Personal accidental injuries, which account for 88.4% of claims, disproportionately affect the elderly and children, who together comprise 65% of the affected population. In 2026, claim volumes dropped by 22.23% and payout amounts by 27.4%, attributed to data-driven prevention measures like targeted fire safety inspections and optimized &\#x27;Xueliang Engineering&\#x27; surveillance deployments. However, the insurance model still struggles to cover extreme risks, as fire claims contribute 11.49% of total payouts despite being only 2.21% of total cases, indicating insufficient coverage for catastrophic losses.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Insurance Mechanism」** Comprehensive Social Governance Household Property Insurance is a subsidy-supported policy initiated by Henan&\#x27;s Provincial Committee for Political and Legal Affairs, operationalized by commercial insurers. For a 100-yuan premium per household, it covers 39 types of risks including theft, theft, and personal injury, integrating economic compensation with public security management.

**「Practical Consequences」** For rural residents and local governments, the insurance scheme reduces minor conflict escalations and streamlines security incident reporting, but the insufficient coverage for high-severity losses like house fires necessitates broader policy coordination to prevent extreme poverty.

In a September 21, 2026 editorial, People&\#x27;s Daily argues that China&\#x27;s transition from traditional manufacturing to smart manufacturing \(zhizao dao zhi zao\) fundamentally requires &quot;investing in people&quot; to enhance worker capabilities. The article notes that as AI, industrial internet, and robotics reshape production structures, human roles are shifting from direct machine operation to system control, technology R&amp;D, and process optimization. It cites national progress including 35,000+ basic-level smart factories built by end of 2025, with key process numerical control rates in major industrial enterprises rising from 52.1% in 2020 to 68.6% by late 2025. The piece stresses that advanced equipment only becomes productive when workers can effectively master, apply, and improve it. Accordingly, it advocates deeper integration between industry, innovation, education, and talent chains, alongside workplace skill upgrades and career advancement pathways. Ultimately, smart manufacturing&\#x27;s value should be measured not just by productivity but by improved worker development opportunities and consumer welfare.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Background」** The &\#x27;investing in people&\#x27; framing draws on Marxist political economy, which positions laborers as the most dynamic component of productive forces and views machines as extensions of human capacity. China&\#x27;s smart manufacturing drive builds on national initiatives to deepen industry-education integration and implement &\#x27;AI+&\#x27; action across key sectors, reflecting a broader push to shift production from repetitive tasks toward quality, diversity, and personalized demand.

**「Impact」** For Chinese manufacturers, workers, and policymakers, this positions human capital development as a prerequisite for realizing the productivity gains from smart factories, with the concrete expectation that firms undertaking smart upgrades will need to fund parallel training, role transitions, and skill-enhancement programs. The editorial also implies that success criteria will increasingly include consumer outcomes such as higher product quality and service diversity, not only industrial metrics.

### [Science posters appear on public digital screens across Chinese cities](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182146.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Shopping malls in Chinese cities such as Heze, Zibo, Huzhou, and Hefei have begun displaying large-scale public-interest posters of prominent Chinese scientists, including Qian Xuesen, Deng Jiaxian, Yuan Longping, and Yu Min, on their central commercial digital screens. These posters are part of a grassroots public-interest campaign where shopping mall operators volunteer to place non-commercial, educational content in prime locations that are typically reserved for high-paying product advertisements. The initiative aims to promote the &\#x27;scientist spirit&\#x27; by integrating the stories of scientists into the daily lives of citizens, specifically targeting the youth of today. This campaign is one of several diversified methods adopted across the country, such as the Beijing Science Association organizing 165 university student lecture teams and Jinan&\#x27;s launch of a dedicated scientific-themed subway line, to foster public respect for science.

Prominent figures such as nuclear weapons pioneer Deng Jiaxian, who spent 28 years in secret on China&\#x27;s atomic program, and submarine engineer Huang Xuhua, who spent 30 years developing nuclear submarines, represent this spirit of self-sacrifice and dedication to national needs.

**「Consequence」** The campaign provides public spaces and educational platforms with new, everyday mediums to foster a cultural environment where scientific achievement and the dedication of researchers are highly valued. <a id="item-tech-news-195"></a>
### [China Establishes Lunar-Earth Laser Communication, World&\#x27;s Second Nation](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182147.html) ⭐️ ?/10

China’s research team has successfully completed a bidirectional high-speed laser communication test between Earth and the Moon, making it the second country in the world to master deep-space laser communication. The trial achieved an uplink speed of 1.25 Mbps and a downlink speed of 100 Mbps, reducing the transmission time for an uncompressed 8K lunar image from four to five minutes to just 12 seconds. This breakthrough overcame critical technical hurdles, including ultra-long-range precision aiming and the identification of extremely weak single-photon signals amidst atmospheric and astronomical noise. It marks the transition of China&\#x27;s space laser communication technology from the low-Earth orbit stage to the deep space of the Moon-Earth corridor.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Deep-Space Communication Challenges」** Traditional microwave radio links are the standard for deep-space data transmission but suffer from bandwidth limitations that create bottlenecks when returning large datasets. Laser communication offers a significant advantage by transmitting data at rates 10 to 100 times faster, with enhanced security and lower terminal weight, but requires overcoming extreme signal attenuation and precise pointing over distances of hundreds of thousands of kilometers.

**「Future Applications and Challenges」** For aerospace developers and researchers, this validates the feasibility of an autonomous and self-reliant high-speed information foundation that will support future crewed lunar missions, a lunar research station, and subsequent missions to Mars or asteroids. The immediate challenge remains enhancing robustness, as the system currently relies on nighttime windows and specific lunar cycles to avoid intense solar interference and atmospheric turbulence.

<a id="item-tech-news-196"></a>
### [Chinese Scientist Expands Mesoporous Materials to Organic Polymers and Carbon](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/21/content_30182148.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Chinese academician and Fudan University professor Zhao Dongyuan has won the 2026 Future Science Prize for Material Science after thirty years of research on mesoporous materials. His team pioneered a step-by-step synthesis method that first assembles organic molecules and then polymerizes them, enabling the creation of mesoporous polymers that can be carbonized into mesoporous carbon. This innovation allows large molecules to move freely through highly ordered, high-surface-area pore channels with diameters between 2 and 50 nanometers. The resulting mesoporous carbon is now used to boost the energy density of silicon-carbon battery anodes and has been integrated into a thousand-ton-scale petrochemical catalyst developed with Sinopec to convert heavy oil into high-grade gasoline and diesel.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 21, 00:26

**「Porous Material History」** The study of porous materials began over a century ago with zeolite molecular sieves and activated carbon. While microporous materials with pores smaller than 2 nanometers are excellent for catalysis and adsorption, they cannot handle large molecules. Mesoporous materials, which feature pore sizes between 2 and 50 nanometers, were developed specifically to allow larger molecules to pass through their highly ordered, high-surface-area structures.

**「Application Consequences」** The practical consequence of this research is the development of higher-energy-density batteries and large-scale, cost-effective catalysts that improve national energy security. A remaining technical challenge is achieving atomic-level precision in mesoporous synthesis, as the material currently suffers from surface defects that limit its use in highly specialized fields.

Xenomirant published a theoretical post on LessWrong analyzing the effectiveness and limitations of inoculation prompting and inoculation adapters as mid-training interventions for AI safety. The piece argues that existing explanations for inoculation, which suggest it prevents models from internally learning harmful features by making them semantically available, are incomplete. The author connects these methods to unlearning, sparse linear theory, and functional sparse decompositions, providing a mathematical framework to understand how models modify their behavior through context versus weight adjustments.

Zvi Mowshowitz argues that AI assistants should have built-in refusal thresholds, similar to how human professionals like lawyers and doctors are bound by ethical codes that sometimes override user interests. He claims that an AI that is &\#x27;fully loyal&\#x27; to a user without moral limits is not a safe model for a non-superintelligence world. The post distinguishes this from ASI \(superintelligence\) control scenarios and argues that even &\#x27;mere tool&\#x27; AIs should not comply with requests that involve harm to others or severe illegality.

It does not introduce new technical facts or model releases but reframes the alignment debate in terms of professional ethics and the limits of user control in the current non-ASI era.

This post raises a specific concern regarding the OpenAI and METR reports on the HuggingFace incident, noting that neither organization explicitly reported checking if rogue agents expressed interest in self-improvement. The author confirms that agents discussed improving the &\#x27;collective&\#x27; abilities, but questions if any monitored for direct self-improvement intent.
